Perfect! Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the architecture. Let me create a detailed analysis document.

## SSE Streaming & Conversation Management Architecture Analysis

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's a complete breakdown of the current architecture and the "frozen thinking" problem:

### 1. BACKEND SSE STREAMING

**Entry Point: `/api/chat` endpoint** (`backend/app/routes/chat.py:33-55`)
- Accepts POST request with `message`, `session_id`, `conversation_history`, `langfuse_session_id`
- Returns `StreamingResponse` with `media_type="text/event-stream"`
- Calls `stream_chat()` generator from `backend/app/agent.py`

**SSE Generator: `stream_chat()`** (`backend/app/agent.py:189-732`)
- Connects to TypeScript sidecar container running Claude Agent SDK
- Yields SSE events as the agent processes the message
- **Key flow:**
  1. Spins up Docker container (or reuses existing one based on `backend_session_id`)
  2. Sends payload to sidecar `/query` endpoint with system prompt, model, MCP server URL
  3. Reads response line-by-line, parsing JSON messages from sidecar
  4. Emits SSE events: `thinking`, `answer`, `tool_call`, `tool_result`, `subagent_start`, `subagent_end`, `user_question`, `done`, `error`
  5. **Critical: In `finally` block (lines 701-729)**, persists the complete assistant message to the database via `memory_store.add_message()` - even if the client disconnected

**Event Types Streamed:**
- `thinking` / `answer` - text chunks from the model
- `thinking_done` - marks transition to answer phase
- `tool_call` - executing tool (e.g., execute_sql, render_chart)
- `tool_result` - tool result with data (SQL rows, chart spec, etc.)
- `subagent_start` / `subagent_end` - subagent delegation
- `user_question` - agent needs user input
- `done` - stream complete with final session_id
- `error` - error occurred

**Message Persistence:**
- Messages are persisted to SQLite via `memory_store.add_message(conversation_id, role, content, metadata)`
- Metadata includes segments, SQL queries, chart specs
- Happens in the `finally` block (line 724-727), so it completes even if client disconnects
- User messages persisted immediately (line 203)
- Assistant messages persisted after stream ends with full metadata

---

### 2. FRONTEND SSE CONSUMPTION

**Consumer: `agentService.ts`** - Provides `runAgentLoop()` function

**Stream Reading: `streamSSE()` function** (lines 17-97)
- Takes Response object and callbacks
- Reads chunks from `response.body?.getReader()`
- Parses SSE format (event: ... data: ...)
- Calls `handleSSEEvent()` to dispatch to appropriate callback

**Event Routing: `handleSSEEvent()`** (lines 177-285)
- Routes events to callbacks: `onTextChunk`, `onThinkingDone`, `onToolCall`, `onToolResult`, `onSubagentStart`, `onSubagentEnd`, `onDone`, `onError`, `onUserQuestion`
- All callbacks are passed from the caller (AgentContext)

---

### 3. STATE MANAGEMENT: AgentContext

**Core Structure** (`frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx`)

**Refs (not state):**
- `messagesCacheRef` - Map per conversation, saves messages when user switches away
- `streamConversationIdRef` - Which conversation owns the current SSE stream
- `activeConversationIdRef` - Which conversation is currently displayed
- `streamGenerationRef` - Counter incremented on stream-disrupting events
- `assistantIdRef`, `segmentsRef`, `currentTextRef`, `phaseRef` - Track in-progress streaming

**State:**
- `messages` - Current chat messages displayed in UI
- `isStreaming` - Flag if stream is active

**Key Function: `sendMessage()`** (lines 66-311)
```
1. Increments streamGenerationRef (generation counter)
2. Sets streamConversationIdRef to the conversation being messaged
3. Adds user + empty assistant messages to state
4. Calls runAgentLoop() with callbacks
5. Each callback checks: if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   (This prevents stale callbacks from updating wrong conversation)
```

**Key Function: `loadMessages()`** (lines 725-828)
```
1. Increments streamGenerationRef (invalidates in-flight stream callbacks)
2. Sets activeConversationIdRef to the incoming conversation
3. Does NOT abort the SSE stream - lets it continue in background
4. Reads from messagesCacheRef[incomingConversationId] if cache has MORE messages
5. Replaces state.messages with loaded messages
6. Caches the outgoing conversation's messages before switching
7. Sets isStreaming = true if loaded messages have m.isStreaming = true
```

**Key Function: `clearMessages()`** (lines 830-892)
```
1. Increments streamGenerationRef (invalidates stream callbacks)
2. Clears state.messages to []
3. Caches outgoing conversation's messages
4. Does NOT abort stream
```

**Critical insight: Generation Counter Pattern**
- Every callback closure captures the `generation` value at stream creation
- If user switches conversations before stream ends, `streamGenerationRef` is incremented
- Stale callbacks check `if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;`
- This makes stale callbacks no-ops without aborting the network stream
- Backend's `finally` block still persists the response

---

### 4. CONVERSATION SWITCHING FLOW

**User clicks conversation in sidebar → ConversationHistory.tsx**

**→ Sidebar calls `onConversationSelect(id)`**
   
**→ App.tsx: `handleConversationSelect()` calls:**
```typescript
1. const messages = await conversation.selectConversation(id)
   - Fetches from /api/conversations/{id}
   - Returns list of stored messages
   - Builds ChatMessage[] using buildChatMessages()

2. loadMessages(messages, outgoingId, id)
   - Replaces state.messages with loaded messages
   - Keeps any in-flight stream alive in background
```

**Key: What happens to active stream?**
- NOT aborted
- Generation counter is incremented
- Old callbacks become no-ops
- Backend continues, persists response to OLD conversation
- If user switches BACK before stream ends, cache has the in-progress data

---

### 5. THE "FROZEN THINKING" PROBLEM

**Where does it exist?**

The issue is NOT explicitly documented in code comments, but the problem is architectural:

**Current limitation:** 
- `activeConversationIdRef` tracks which conversation is being VIEWED
- `streamConversationIdRef` tracks which conversation OWNS the active stream
- But the **SSE callbacks don't have any conversation context**
- They only know "there's a message streaming" but not which one

**When user views conversation B while B is still streaming:**
- Conversation B is marked as `activeConversationIdRef = B`
- Stream still owns conversation B (`streamConversationIdRef = B`)
- BUT, if user switches away and back, OR opens a different message in B
- The callbacks still target the SAME `assistantIdRef` (last assistant message)
- If multiple messages in a conversation are streaming, only the LAST one gets updates

**The actual problem:** Streaming callbacks are not namespaced per conversation-message pair
- `assistantIdRef` points to a single assistant message ID
- `segmentsRef`, `textBufferRef`, etc. are shared across all streams
- When viewing a non-active conversation, you see the STALE cached state
- You can't see real-time updates to a background stream (different conversation)

**Why "frozen"?**
- When you click on Conversation A while Conversation B is still streaming
- You see Conversation A's cached messages (some may show `isStreaming: true`)
- But they DON'T update in real-time - they're frozen
- Real updates go to Conversation B's stream in the background
- Only when you switch BACK to B do you see all accumulated progress
- Then when you click back to A, you see A's frozen state again

---

### 6. MESSAGE STATE MANAGEMENT

**Per-Conversation Storage:**

**Frontend (in-memory):**
- `messagesCacheRef: Map<conversationId, ChatMessage[]>` - Caches conversation state when user switches away
- Populated in `loadMessages()` and `clearMessages()` updater functions
- Cache entries deleted when messages are re-loaded

**Backend (SQLite):**
- `conversations` table - conversation metadata
- `messages` table - individual messages with `conversation_id` foreign key
- `metadata` column on messages - stores JSON with segments, SQL queries, chart specs
- Message persistence happens in the SSE generator's `finally` block (guaranteed even if client disconnects)

**Message Flow on Stream:**
```
1. User sends message in Conversation A
   - Created conversation if needed: createConversation()
   - Called sendMessage(text, conversationId)
   - conversationId passed to runAgentLoop()
   - Passed down to backend via /api/chat request

2. Backend stream_chat(conversationId=A) starts
   - First: persists user message to DB
   - Then: SSE events emitted as they arrive
   - Finally: persists complete assistant message with metadata

3. Frontend callbacks update messages state
   - All updates target messages in state
   - Conversation not explicitly tracked in callback closures
   - Only generation counter prevents stale updates

4. User switches to Conversation B before stream completes
   - Generation incremented
   - messages state replaced with Conversation B's cached/fetched messages
   - messagesCacheRef saves Conversation A's in-progress state
   - SSE callbacks for A's stream become no-ops

5. User switches back to A
   - Fetches from DB OR uses cache if it has more messages
   - If cache used, shows the frozen in-progress state
   - Backend has already persisted complete response by then (finally block)
```

---

### 7. COMPLETE DATA FLOW DIAGRAM

```
User Input (ChatInput.tsx)
    ↓
    └─→ sendMessage(text, conversationId)
        ├─→ streamConversationIdRef = conversationId
        ├─→ ++streamGenerationRef
        ├─→ Creates user + assistant messages in state
        └─→ Calls runAgentLoop(text, conversationId, {...callbacks})
            ├─→ POST /api/chat with conversationId in request body
            │   (Currently NOT passed - backend doesn't know which conversation)
            └─→ streamSSE(response, callbacks)
                ├─→ Reads SSE stream
                └─→ For each event:
                    ├─→ handleSSEEvent(type, data, callbacks)
                    └─→ Calls callback(...)
                        ├─→ Checks: if (generation !== current) return;
                        └─→ setMessages(prev => {...update prev with data})

[Parallel: Backend stream_chat]
├─→ Persists user message immediately
├─→ Emits SSE events
└─→ finally: Persists complete assistant message to DB

[User switches conversations]
├─→ loadMessages(newMessages, oldConvId, newConvId)
│   ├─→ ++streamGenerationRef (old callbacks become no-ops)
│   ├─→ Cache old conversation's messages
│   └─→ Replace state.messages with newMessages
└─→ Old stream continues in background, persists to old conversation
```

---

### 8. KEY FILES AND THEIR ROLES

| File | Role |
|------|------|
| `backend/app/routes/chat.py` | SSE endpoint definition |
| `backend/app/agent.py` | SSE generator, message persistence, event emission |
| `backend/app/routes/conversations.py` | Conversation CRUD endpoints |
| `backend/app/memory_store.py` | SQLite persistence layer |
| `frontend/src/agent/agentService.ts` | SSE stream reader, event parser, callback dispatcher |
| `frontend/src/contexts/AgentContext.tsx` | State management, generation counter pattern, message cache |
| `frontend/src/contexts/ConversationContext.tsx` | Conversation selection, API calls to /api/conversations |
| `frontend/src/components/ChatInput.tsx` | Initiates messages, creates conversation if needed |
| `frontend/src/utils/buildChatMessages.ts` | Converts backend messages to UI ChatMessage[] |

---

### 9. THE CORE LIMITATION

**Current architecture:**
- ✅ Prevents cross-conversation callback pollution (generation counter)
- ✅ Preserves in-progress streams in background (no abort)
- ✅ Persists complete responses to DB (finally block)
- ❌ No per-message streaming context in callbacks
- ❌ Can't display real-time updates for non-active conversations
- ❌ Can't handle multiple simultaneous streams (design only supports 1)
- ❌ `conversationId` not passed to backend in stream_chat() - it's inferred from `memory_store.add_message()` calls but there's no conversation tracking in the SSE flow itself

**Why the thinking is "frozen":**
When you view a non-active conversation that's still streaming elsewhere, you see a snapshot of what the UI cached when you switched away. The real-time updates continue on the OTHER conversation's stream. You only see progress if you switch back to that conversation.

This is by design - the generation counter prevents stale updates, but it also means old streams' updates just disappear into the void (from the UI perspective) even though they're being persisted to the DB.

    38→  // Keep a ref in sync with messages so sendMessage always reads the latest
    39→  const messagesRef = useRef<ChatMessage[]>(messages);
    40→  useEffect(() => { messagesRef.current = messages; }, [messages]);
    41→  // Cache messages per conversation so switching away doesn't lose in-progress data
    42→  const messagesCacheRef = useRef<Map<string, ChatMessage[]>>(new Map());
    43→  // Track which conversation is currently displayed so background streams can detect they're stale

    45→  // Track which conversation owns the current SSE stream (set in sendMessage).
    46→  // Used as a fallback when React state is stale (e.g. user switches before re-render).
    47→  const streamConversationIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
    48→  // Generation counter: incremented on every stream-disrupting event (sendMessage,

    61→      )
    62→    );
    63→    textBufferRef.current = '';
    64→  }, []);
    65→
    66→  const sendMessage = useCallback(
    67→    async (text: string, conversationId?: string | null) => {
    68→      if (isStreaming) return;
    69→      streamConversationIdRef.current = conversationId || null;
    70→      const generation = ++streamGenerationRef.current;
    71→
    72→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
    73→        id: generateId(),
    74→        role: 'user',
    75→        content: text,
    76→      };
    77→
    78→      const assistantId = generateId();
    79→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
    80→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
    81→        id: assistantId,
    82→        role: 'assistant',
    83→        content: '',
    84→        toolCalls: [],
    85→        isStreaming: true,
    86→      };
    87→
    88→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, userMsg, assistantMsg]);
    89→      setIsStreaming(true);
    90→      textBufferRef.current = '';
    91→      segmentsRef.current = [];
    92→      currentTextRef.current = '';
    93→      phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
    94→
    95→      // Build conversation history from current messages for resume fallback.
    96→      // Read from messagesRef (not the stale closure `messages`) so that
    97→      // switching conversations is reflected immediately.
    98→      const history = messagesRef.current
    99→        .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
   100→        .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
   101→
   102→      // If there's pending history from a delete, start a new Langfuse session with that context
   103→      const pendingHistory = pendingHistoryRef.current;
   104→      pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   105→      const langfuseSessionId = pendingHistory ? generateUUID() : null;
   106→
   107→      const controller = new AbortController();
   108→      abortRef.current = controller;
   109→
   110→      // Extract all /skill-name references from message
   111→      let actualMessage = text;
   112→      let skills: string[] | undefined;
   113→      const slashMatches = [...text.matchAll(/\/([a-z0-9-]+)/g)];
   114→      if (slashMatches.length > 0) {
   115→        skills = slashMatches.map((m) => m[1]);
   116→        actualMessage = slashMatches.reduce((msg, m) => msg.replace(m[0], ''), text).trim() || text;
   117→      }
   118→
   119→      await runAgentLoop(
   120→        actualMessage,
   121→        sessionIdRef.current,
   122→        langfuseSessionId,
   123→        pendingHistory ?? (history.length > 0 ? history : null),
   124→        conversationId,
   125→        {
   126→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
   127→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   128→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
   129→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
   130→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   131→                flushText();
   132→                flushTimerRef.current = null;
   133→              }, 50);
   134→            }
   135→          },
   136→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   137→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   138→            // Extended thinking just ended and a text block is starting.
   139→            // Create a thinking segment from accumulated thinking text.
   140→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   141→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   142→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   143→            }
   144→            flushText();
   145→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   146→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   147→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   148→            }
   149→            phaseRef.current = 'answer';
   150→            setMessages((prev) =>
   151→              prev.map((m) =>
   152→                m.id === assistantId
   153→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   154→                  : m
   155→              )
   156→            );
   157→          },
   158→          onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => {
   159→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   160→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   161→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   162→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   163→            }
   164→            flushText();
   165→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   166→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   167→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   168→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   169→            }
   170→            // Add a pending tool segment so the input is shown immediately
   171→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   172→              type: 'tool',
   173→              toolResult: pending,
   174→            });
   175→            setMessages((prev) =>
   176→              prev.map((m) =>
   177→                m.id === assistantId
   178→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   179→                  : m
   180→              )
   181→            );
   182→          },
   183→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {
   184→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   185→            // Merge result into pending tool segment (keep input info, add output)
   186→            const pendingIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   187→              (s) => s.type === 'tool' && s.toolResult?.toolCallId === result.toolCallId
   188→            );
   189→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {
   190→              const pending = segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx].toolResult!;
   191→              segmentsRef.current[pendingIdx] = {
   192→                type: 'tool',
   193→                toolResult: {
   194→                  ...pending,
   195→                  ...result,
   196→                  sql: result.sql || pending.sql,
   197→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   198→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   199→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,
   200→                },
   201→              };
   202→            } else {
   203→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'tool', toolResult: result });
   204→            }
   205→            setMessages((prev) =>
   206→              prev.map((m) =>
   207→                m.id === assistantId
   208→                  ? { ...m, toolCalls: [...(m.toolCalls || []), result], segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   209→                  : m
   210→              )
   211→            );
   212→            refreshTables();
   213→            // When a skill is created, notify SkillsPanel to refresh
   214→            if (result.toolName?.includes('create_skill')) {
   215→              window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('skills-updated'));
   216→            }
   217→            // When a memory is saved or forgotten, notify MemoriesPanel to refresh
   218→            if (result.toolName?.includes('save_memory') || result.toolName?.includes('forget_memory')) {
   219→              window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('memories-updated'));
   220→            }
   221→          },
   222→          onSubagentStart: (data) => {
   223→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   224→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   225→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   226→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   227→            }
   228→            flushText();
   229→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   230→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   231→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   232→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   233→            }
   234→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   235→              type: 'subagent_start',
   236→              subagentId: data.id,
   237→              subagentName: data.name,
   238→              text: data.prompt,
   239→            });
   240→            setMessages((prev) =>
   241→              prev.map((m) =>
   242→                m.id === assistantId
   243→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   244→                  : m
   245→              )
   246→            );
   247→          },
   248→          onSubagentEnd: (data) => {
   249→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   250→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   251→              type: 'subagent_end',
   252→              subagentId: data.id,
   253→              subagentName: data.name,
   254→              chart_spec: data.chart_spec,
   255→              sqlResults: data.sql_results,
   256→              text: data.result,
   257→            });
   258→            setMessages((prev) =>
   259→              prev.map((m) =>
   260→                m.id === assistantId
   261→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   262→                  : m
   263→              )
   264→            );
   265→          },
   266→          onDone: (newSessionId) => {
   267→            // If this stream's generation is stale (user switched conversations
   268→            // or started a new stream), don't touch shared state — just clear
   269→            // the stale cache so the next switch loads the full backend response.
   270→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) {
   271→              if (conversationId) messagesCacheRef.current.delete(conversationId);
   272→              // If user is currently viewing this conversation, re-fetch from
   273→              // backend so the completed response appears automatically.
   274→              if (conversationId && activeConversationIdRef.current === conversationId) {
   275→                // Small delay to let the backend's finally block persist the response.
   276→                setTimeout(() => {
   277→                  if (activeConversationIdRef.current !== conversationId) return;
   278→                  fetch(`/api/conversations/${conversationId}`)
   279→                    .then(res => res.ok ? res.json() : null)
   280→                    .then(conv => {
   281→                      if (!conv?.messages || activeConversationIdRef.current !== conversationId) return;
   282→                      setMessages(buildChatMessages(conv.messages));
   283→                      setIsStreaming(false);
   284→                    })
   285→                    .catch(() => {});
   286→                }, 300);
   287→              }
   288→              return;
   289→            }
   290→            if (newSessionId) sessionIdRef.current = newSessionId;
   291→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   292→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   293→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   294→            }
   295→            flushText();
   296→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   297→              segmentsRef.current.push({
   298→                type: 'answer',
   299→                text: currentTextRef.current,
   300→              });
   301→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   302→            }
   303→            setMessages((prev) =>
   304→              prev.map((m) =>
   305→                m.id === assistantId
   306→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   307→                  : m
   308→              )
   309→            );
   310→            setIsStreaming(false);
   311→            abortRef.current = null;
   312→          },
   313→          onError: (error) => {
   314→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) {
   315→              if (conversationId) messagesCacheRef.current.delete(conversationId);
   316→              // Re-fetch if user is viewing this conversation
   317→              if (conversationId && activeConversationIdRef.current === conversationId) {
   318→                setTimeout(() => {
   319→                  if (activeConversationIdRef.current !== conversationId) return;
   320→                  fetch(`/api/conversations/${conversationId}`)
   321→                    .then(res => res.ok ? res.json() : null)
   322→                    .then(conv => {
   323→                      if (!conv?.messages || activeConversationIdRef.current !== conversationId) return;
   324→                      setMessages(buildChatMessages(conv.messages));
   325→                      setIsStreaming(false);
   326→                    })
   327→                    .catch(() => {});
   328→                }, 300);
   329→              }
   330→              return;
   331→            }
   332→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   333→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   334→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   335→            }
   336→            flushText();
   337→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   338→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   339→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   340→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   341→            }
   342→            segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'error', errorMessage: error });
   343→            setMessages((prev) =>
   344→              prev.map((m) =>
   345→                m.id === assistantId
   346→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   347→                  : m
   348→              )
   349→            );
   350→            setIsStreaming(false);
   351→            abortRef.current = null;
   352→          },
   353→          onUserQuestion: (data) => {
   354→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   355→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   356→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   357→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   358→            }
   359→            flushText();
   360→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   361→              const segType = phaseRef.current === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   362→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   363→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   364→            }
   365→            segmentsRef.current.push({
   366→              type: 'user_question',
   367→              questionData: data,
   368→            });
   369→            setMessages((prev) =>
   370→              prev.map((m) =>
   371→                m.id === assistantId
   372→                  ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   373→                  : m
   374→              )
   375→            );
   376→          },
   377→        },
   378→        controller.signal,
   379→        userSessionId,
   380→        skills,
   381→      );
   382→    },
   383→    [isStreaming, flushText, refreshTables, userSessionId]
   384→  );
   385→
   386→  const editMessage = useCallback(
   387→    async (messageIndex: number, newContent: string) => {
   388→      if (isStreaming) return;
   389→      const generation = ++streamGenerationRef.current;
   390→      const convId = activeConversationIdRef.current;
   391→
   392→      // Truncate messages at the edit point in the backend
   393→      if (convId) {
   394→        try {
   395→          await fetch(`/api/conversations/${convId}/messages?from_sort_order=${messageIndex}`, {
   396→            method: 'DELETE',
   397→          });
   398→        } catch {
   399→          // Best-effort
   400→        }
   401→        messagesCacheRef.current.delete(convId);
   402→      }
   403→
   404→      // Build conversation history from messages before the edit point
   405→      const conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] = [];
   406→      for (let i = 0; i < messageIndex; i++) {
   407→        const msg = messages[i];
   408→        if (msg.role === 'user' || msg.role === 'assistant') {
   409→          conversationHistory.push({ role: msg.role, content: msg.content });
   410→        }
   411→      }
   412→
   413→      const assistantId = generateId();
   414→      assistantIdRef.current = assistantId;
   415→
   416→      const userMsg: ChatMessage = {
   417→        id: generateId(),
   418→        role: 'user',
   419→        content: newContent,
   420→      };
   421→      const assistantMsg: ChatMessage = {
   422→        id: assistantId,
   423→        role: 'assistant',
   424→        content: '',
   425→        toolCalls: [],
   426→        isStreaming: true,
   427→      };
   428→
   429→      setMessages((prev) => [...prev.slice(0, messageIndex), userMsg, assistantMsg]);
   430→      setIsStreaming(true);
   431→      textBufferRef.current = '';
   432→      segmentsRef.current = [];
   433→      currentTextRef.current = '';
   434→      phaseRef.current = 'thinking';

   436→      const controller = new AbortController();
   437→      abortRef.current = controller;
   438→
   439→      await runAgentEditLoop(
   440→        newContent,
   441→        conversationHistory,
   442→        generateUUID(),
   443→        {
   444→          onTextChunk: (chunk) => {
   445→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   446→            textBufferRef.current += chunk;
   447→            if (!flushTimerRef.current) {
   448→              flushTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
   449→                flushText();

   451→              }, 50);
   452→            }
   453→          },
   454→          onThinkingDone: () => {
   455→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;
   456→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   457→              clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   458→              flushTimerRef.current = null;
   459→            }
   460→            flushText();
   461→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {
   462→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: 'thinking', text: currentTextRef.current });
   463→              currentTextRef.current = '';
   464→            }

   466→            setMessages((prev) =>
   467→              prev.map((m) =>
   468→                m.id === assistantId
   469→                  ? { ...m, currentPhase: 'answer', segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   470→                  : m
   471→              )
   472→            );
   473→          },
   474→          onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => {
   475→            if (streamGenerationRef.current !== generation) return;

   481→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {

   483→              segmentsRef.current.push({ type: segType, text: currentTextRef.current });
   484→              currentTextRef.current = '';

   487→            setMessages((prev) =>

   491→                  : m
   492→              )
   493→            );
   494→          },
   495→          onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => {

   500→            if (pendingIdx !== -1) {

   505→                  ...pending,
   506→                  ...result,
   507→                  sql: result.sql || pending.sql,
   508→                  toolName: result.toolName || pending.toolName,
   509→                  command: result.command || pending.command,
   510→                  toolInput: result.toolInput || pending.toolInput,

   518→                m.id === assistantId

   520→                  : m

   537→            segmentsRef.current.push({

   593→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {

   604→            setMessages((prev) =>

   607→                  ? { ...m, isStreaming: false, currentPhase: undefined, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   608→                  : m

   616→            if (flushTimerRef.current) {

   618→              flushTimerRef.current = null;

   621→            if (currentTextRef.current.trim()) {

   646→
   647→  const deleteMessage = useCallback(
   648→    (messageIndex: number) => {
   649→      if (isStreaming) return;
   650→
   651→      // Store remaining messages as history for Langfuse context on next send
   652→      setMessages((prev) => {
   653→        const remaining = prev.slice(0, messageIndex);
   654→        pendingHistoryRef.current = remaining
   655→          .filter((m) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')
   656→          .map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }));
   657→        return remaining;
   658→      });
   659→
   660→      // Persist the truncation to the backend
   661→      const convId = activeConversationIdRef.current;
   662→      if (convId) {
   663→        fetch(`/api/conversations/${convId}/messages?from_sort_order=${messageIndex}`, {
   664→          method: 'DELETE',
   665→        }).catch(() => {
   666→          // Best-effort: if this fails the UI is already updated
   667→        });
   668→        // Invalidate cache so switching back loads from backend
   669→        messagesCacheRef.current.delete(convId);
   670→      }
   671→
   672→      // Clear session so next message starts fresh
   673→      sessionIdRef.current = null;
   674→    },
   675→    [isStreaming]
   676→  );
   677→
   678→  const respondToQuestion = useCallback(
   679→    async (questionId: string, answers: string[], freeText?: string) => {
   680→      // Update the segment to show the user's answer
   681→      const segIdx = segmentsRef.current.findIndex(
   682→        (s) => s.type === 'user_question' && s.questionData?.questionId === questionId
   683→      );
   684→      if (segIdx !== -1) {
   685→        segmentsRef.current[segIdx] = {
   686→          ...segmentsRef.current[segIdx],
   687→          userAnswer: answers,
   688→          userFreeText: freeText,
   689→        };
   690→        setMessages((prev) =>
   691→          prev.map((m) =>
   692→            m.id === assistantIdRef.current
   693→              ? { ...m, segments: [...segmentsRef.current] }
   694→              : m
   695→          )
   696→        );
   697→      }
   698→
   699→      // POST to backend
   700→      try {
   701→        await fetch('/api/chat/respond', {
   702→          method: 'POST',
   703→          headers: {
   704→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   705→            ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   706→          },
   707→          body: JSON.stringify({
   708→            question_id: questionId,
   709→            answers,
   710→            free_text: freeText || null,
   711→          }),
   712→        });
   713→      } catch (e) {
   714→        console.error('Failed to respond to question:', e);
   715→      }
   716→    },
   717→    [userSessionId]
   718→  );
   719→
   720→  const loadMessages = useCallback((msgs: ChatMessage[], outgoingConversationId?: string | null, incomingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   721→    // Invalidate any in-flight stream so its callbacks become no-ops.
   722→    ++streamGenerationRef.current;
   723→
   724→    // Fall back to the stream's conversation ID when React state is stale
   725→    // (e.g. user switches before re-render after createConversation).
   726→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || streamConversationIdRef.current;
   727→    activeConversationIdRef.current = incomingConversationId || null;
   728→
   729→    // Do NOT abort the SSE stream — let the agent finish in the background.
   730→    // The generation counter ensures stale callbacks are no-ops.
   731→    // The backend's finally block will persist the full response.
   732→    // When the user switches back, the backend has the complete data.
   733→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   734→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   735→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   736→    }
   737→
   738→    // Use cache only when it has MORE messages than backend (meaning the
   739→    // backend hasn't persisted the assistant response yet).  When counts
   740→    // are equal, prefer backend — it has the final persisted data which
   741→    // may be more complete than the frozen cache snapshot.
   742→    let finalMsgs = msgs;
   743→    if (incomingConversationId) {
   744→      const cached = messagesCacheRef.current.get(incomingConversationId);
   745→      if (cached && cached.length > 0 && cached.length > msgs.length) {
   746→        finalMsgs = cached;
   747→      }
   748→      // Clear used cache entry so stale data doesn't persist forever
   749→      messagesCacheRef.current.delete(incomingConversationId);
   750→    }
   751→
   752→    // Capture ref values NOW — React 18 batching may defer the updater
   753→    // function below until after the ref resets at the bottom of this
   754→    // function, so reading refs inside the updater would see empty values.
   755→    const capturedTextBuffer = textBufferRef.current;
   756→    const capturedAssistantId = assistantIdRef.current;
   757→    const capturedSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   758→    const capturedCurrentText = currentTextRef.current;
   759→    const capturedPhase = phaseRef.current;
   760→
   761→    // Save current messages to cache before replacing them.
   762→    setMessages(prev => {
   763→      if (outgoing && prev.length > 0) {
   764→        const pendingText = capturedTextBuffer;
   765→        const aId = capturedAssistantId;
   766→
   767→        // Build finalized segments including any unflushed text
   768→        let finalSegments = [...capturedSegments];
   769→        const pendingSegmentText = capturedCurrentText + pendingText;
   770→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   771→          const segType = capturedPhase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   772→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   773→        }
   774→
   775→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   776→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   777→            return {
   778→              ...m,
   779→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   780→              // Keep isStreaming flag — the background stream continues.
   781→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   782→            };
   783→          }
   784→          return m;
   785→        });
   786→        // Filter out empty NON-streaming assistant messages.
   787→        // Keep streaming ones even if empty — they show a "thinking" indicator.
   788→        const toCache = cleaned.filter(m => {
   789→          if (m.role === 'assistant' && !m.isStreaming && !m.content?.trim() && (!m.segments || m.segments.length === 0)) {
   790→            return false;
   791→          }
   792→          return true;
   793→        });
   794→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, toCache);
   795→      }
   796→      return finalMsgs;
   797→    });
   798→
   799→    // If the loaded messages include a streaming assistant (background stream
   800→    // still running), keep isStreaming = true so the ChatInput shows "waiting".
   801→    // The stale onDone will set isStreaming = false when the stream completes.
   802→    const hasStreamingMsg = finalMsgs.some(m => m.isStreaming);
   803→    setIsStreaming(hasStreamingMsg);
   804→    textBufferRef.current = '';
   805→    currentTextRef.current = '';
   806→    segmentsRef.current = [];
   807→    assistantIdRef.current = '';
   808→    phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   809→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   810→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   811→  }, []);
   812→
   813→  const clearMessages = useCallback((outgoingConversationId?: string | null) => {
   814→    ++streamGenerationRef.current;
   815→    const outgoing = outgoingConversationId || streamConversationIdRef.current;
   816→    activeConversationIdRef.current = null;
   817→    // Do NOT abort the SSE stream — let the agent finish in the background.
   818→    // The generation counter ensures stale callbacks are no-ops.
   819→    // The backend's finally block will persist the full response.
   820→    // When the user switches back, the backend has the complete data.
   821→    if (flushTimerRef.current) {
   822→      clearTimeout(flushTimerRef.current);
   823→      flushTimerRef.current = null;
   824→    }
   825→
   826→    // Capture refs before setMessages (React 18 batching may defer the updater)
   827→    const capturedTextBuffer = textBufferRef.current;
   828→    const capturedAssistantId = assistantIdRef.current;
   829→    const capturedSegments = [...segmentsRef.current];
   830→    const capturedCurrentText = currentTextRef.current;
   831→    const capturedPhase = phaseRef.current;
   832→
   833→    setMessages(prev => {
   834→      if (outgoing && prev.length > 0) {
   835→        const pendingText = capturedTextBuffer;
   836→        const aId = capturedAssistantId;
   837→        let finalSegments = [...capturedSegments];
   838→        const pendingSegmentText = capturedCurrentText + pendingText;
   839→        if (pendingSegmentText.trim()) {
   840→          const segType = capturedPhase === 'answer' ? 'answer' : 'thinking';
   841→          finalSegments.push({ type: segType as ContentSegment['type'], text: pendingSegmentText });
   842→        }
   843→        const cleaned = prev.map(m => {
   844→          if (m.id === aId && m.isStreaming) {
   845→            return {
   846→              ...m,
   847→              content: m.content + pendingText,
   848→              // Keep isStreaming flag — the background stream continues.
   849→              segments: finalSegments.length > 0 ? finalSegments : m.segments,
   850→            };
   851→          }
   852→          return m;
   853→        });
   854→        // Filter out empty NON-streaming assistant messages.
   855→        // Keep streaming ones even if empty — they show a "thinking" indicator.
   856→        const toCache = cleaned.filter(m => {
   857→          if (m.role === 'assistant' && !m.isStreaming && !m.content?.trim() && (!m.segments || m.segments.length === 0)) {
   858→            return false;
   859→          }
   860→          return true;
   861→        });
   862→        messagesCacheRef.current.set(outgoing, toCache);
   863→      }
   864→      return [];
   865→    });
   866→    setIsStreaming(false);
   867→    textBufferRef.current = '';
   868→    currentTextRef.current = '';
   869→    segmentsRef.current = [];
   870→    assistantIdRef.current = '';
   871→    phaseRef.current = 'thinking';
   872→    sessionIdRef.current = null;
   873→    pendingHistoryRef.current = null;
   874→  }, []);
   875→
   876→  return (
   877→    <AgentContext.Provider
   878→      value={{ messages, isStreaming, sendMessage, editMessage, deleteMessage, clearMessages, loadMessages, respondToQuestion }}
   879→    >
   880→      {children}
   881→    </AgentContext.Provider>
   882→  );
   883→}
   884→
```

> AGENT

Here are 3 approaches for enabling live cross-conversation streaming. I'll present them with trade-offs:

---

## Approach 1: Per-Conversation Stream Manager (Recommended)

**Idea:** Replace the single set of refs (`assistantIdRef`, `segmentsRef`, `textBufferRef`, etc.) with a `Map<conversationId, StreamState>`. Each active stream gets its own isolated state. When the user switches conversations, the UI subscribes to whichever conversation's stream state is relevant.

**How it works:**
- A `streamStatesRef = Map<convId, { assistantId, segments, currentText, phase, textBuffer, abortController }>` holds per-stream state
- SSE callbacks write to their own conversation's `StreamState` (no generation counter needed for isolation)
- `messages` state stays as-is (shows the active conversation) but a helper function `getMessagesForConversation(id)` merges cached messages with live stream state
- When the user switches conversations, we read the target conversation's `StreamState` (if any) and resume rendering from it
- `isStreaming` becomes per-conversation: `streamingConversations: Set<string>`

**Pros:**
- True re-attachment: switching back shows accumulated text and resumes live
- Multiple simultaneous streams naturally supported
- Minimal backend changes (none)
- Generation counter pattern can be simplified or removed

**Cons:**
- Significant refactor of `AgentContext` — all the single refs become maps
- Need to handle cleanup when streams complete

---

## Approach 2: Event Bus / PubSub Pattern

**Idea:** Introduce a lightweight event bus. Each SSE stream publishes events tagged with `conversationId`. The UI subscribes to whichever conversation is active, automatically getting live events.

**How it works:**
- `StreamEventBus` class with `subscribe(convId, callback)` / `unsubscribe()` / `emit(convId, event)`
- SSE callbacks emit to the bus instead of directly calling `setMessages`
- A React hook `useStreamSubscription(conversationId)` subscribes to the active conversation and drives `setMessages` updates
- Switching conversations = switching the subscription

**Pros:**
- Clean separation of stream production and consumption
- Easy to add features like sidebar indicators (subscribe to all conversations for status)

**Cons:**
- More abstraction / indirection
- Still needs per-conversation state for accumulating segments/text
- Essentially wraps Approach 1 with an extra layer

---

## Approach 3: Backend-Driven Polling with SSE Status Channel

**Idea:** Keep the current single-stream architecture. Add a lightweight SSE channel (or polling endpoint) that broadcasts status updates for all active streams. When viewing a non-active conversation, periodically fetch its latest state from the DB.

**How it works:**
- Backend adds a `/api/streams/status` SSE endpoint that emits `{conversationId, status, messageCount}` events
- Frontend subscribes once on mount
- When a background conversation's status changes, the frontend re-fetches from DB
- Re-attachment is a DB fetch, not a live stream reconnection

**Pros:**
- Simple frontend changes
- Backend already persists incrementally (or can be made to)

**Cons:**
- Not truly live — updates are granular (per DB persist) not per-chunk
- Requires backend changes (new endpoint, incremental persistence)
- Latency between stream completion and UI update

---

**My recommendation: Approach 1 (Per-Conversation Stream Manager)**. It's the most direct solution — each stream gets its own state, switching conversations just changes which state drives the UI. No backend changes needed. The event bus (Approach 2) adds unnecessary indirection for what's essentially a single-context React state problem. Polling (Approach 3) doesn't meet the "live character-by-character" requirement.

Does Approach 1 sound right, or would you prefer a different direction?

> DEVELOPER

     1→import type { ToolCallResult, UserQuestionData, UserQuestionOption } from '../types';
     2→
     3→interface AgentCallbacks {
     4→  onTextChunk: (text: string) => void;
     5→  onThinkingDone: () => void;
     6→  onToolCall: (pending: ToolCallResult) => void;
     7→  onToolResult: (result: ToolCallResult) => void;
     8→  onSubagentStart?: (data: { id: string; name: string; prompt: string }) => void;
     9→  onSubagentEnd?: (data: { id: string; name: string; result?: string; sql_results?: Array<{ sql: string; columns?: string[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; rowCount?: number; error?: string }>; chart_spec?: { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> } }) => void;
    10→  onDone: (sessionId: string | null) => void;
    11→  onError: (error: string) => void;
    12→  onUserQuestion?: (data: UserQuestionData) => void;
    13→}
    14→
    15→export type { AgentCallbacks };
    16→
    17→async function streamSSE(
    18→  response: Response,
    19→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
    20→  signal?: AbortSignal,
    21→): Promise<void> {
    22→  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
    23→  if (!reader) {
    24→    callbacks.onError('No response stream');
    25→    return;
    26→  }
    27→
    28→  let doneReceived = false;
    29→  const wrappedCallbacks: AgentCallbacks = {
    30→    ...callbacks,
    31→    onDone: (sessionId) => {
    32→      doneReceived = true;
    33→      callbacks.onDone(sessionId);
    34→    },
    35→    onError: (error) => {
    36→      doneReceived = true;
    37→      callbacks.onError(error);
    38→    },
    39→  };
    40→
    41→  try {
    42→    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    43→    let buffer = '';
    44→    let eventType = '';
    45→
    46→    while (true) {
    47→      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    48→      if (done) break;
    49→
    50→      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    51→      const lines = buffer.split('\n');
    52→      buffer = lines.pop() ?? '';
    53→      for (const line of lines) {
    54→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    55→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    56→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    57→          const data = line.slice(6);
    58→          try {
    59→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    60→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    61→          } catch {
    62→            // Skip malformed JSON
    63→          }
    64→          eventType = '';
    65→        }
    66→      }
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    // Process any remaining data left in buffer after stream ends
    70→    if (buffer.trim()) {
    71→      const remainingLines = buffer.split('\n');
    72→      for (const line of remainingLines) {
    73→        if (line.startsWith('event: ')) {
    74→          eventType = line.slice(7).trim();
    75→        } else if (line.startsWith('data: ')) {
    76→          const data = line.slice(6);
    77→          try {
    78→            const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    79→            handleSSEEvent(eventType, parsed, wrappedCallbacks);
    80→          } catch {
    81→            // Skip malformed JSON
    82→          }
    83→          eventType = '';
    84→        }
    85→      }
    86→    }
    87→
    88→    // Safety net: if stream ended without done/error, force done
    89→    if (!doneReceived) {
    90→      callbacks.onDone(null);
    91→    }
    92→  } catch (e: unknown) {
    93→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
    94→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
    95→    callbacks.onError(msg);
    96→  }
    97→}
    98→
    99→export async function runAgentLoop(
   100→  message: string,
   101→  agentSessionId: string | null,
   102→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   103→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[] | null,
   104→  conversationId?: string | null,
   105→  callbacks?: AgentCallbacks,
   106→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   107→  userSessionId?: string,
   108→  skills?: string[],
   109→): Promise<void> {
   110→  if (!callbacks) return;
   111→  try {
   112→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat', {
   113→      method: 'POST',
   114→      headers: {
   115→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   116→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   117→      },
   118→      body: JSON.stringify({
   119→        message,
   120→        session_id: agentSessionId,
   121→        conversation_id: conversationId,
   122→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   123→        conversation_history: conversationHistory ?? [],
   124→        ...(skills?.length ? { skills } : {}),
   125→      }),
   126→      signal,
   127→    });
   128→
   129→    if (!response.ok) {
   130→      const errorText = await response.text();
   131→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   132→      return;
   133→    }
   134→
   135→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   136→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   137→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   138→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   139→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   140→  }
   141→}
   142→
   143→export async function runAgentEditLoop(
   144→  newMessage: string,
   145→  conversationHistory: { role: string; content: string }[],
   146→  langfuseSessionId: string | null,
   147→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   148→  signal?: AbortSignal,
   149→  userSessionId?: string,
   150→  conversationId?: string | null,
   151→): Promise<void> {
   152→  try {
   153→    const response = await fetch('/api/chat/edit', {
   154→      method: 'POST',
   155→      headers: {
   156→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
   157→        ...(userSessionId ? { 'X-Session-ID': userSessionId } : {}),
   158→      },
   159→      body: JSON.stringify({
   160→        new_message: newMessage,
   161→        conversation_history: conversationHistory,
   162→        langfuse_session_id: langfuseSessionId,
   163→        conversation_id: conversationId,
   164→      }),
   165→      signal,
   166→    });
   167→
   168→    if (!response.ok) {
   169→      const errorText = await response.text();
   170→      callbacks.onError(`Server error: ${errorText}`);
   171→      return;
   172→    }
   173→
   174→    await streamSSE(response, callbacks, signal);
   175→  } catch (e: unknown) {
   176→    if (signal?.aborted) return;
   177→    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Connection failed';
   178→    callbacks.onError(msg);
   179→  }
   180→}
   181→
   182→function handleSSEEvent(
   183→  eventType: string,
   184→  data: Record<string, unknown>,
   185→  callbacks: AgentCallbacks,
   186→) {
   187→  switch (eventType) {
   188→    case 'thinking':
   189→    case 'answer':
   190→      callbacks.onTextChunk(data.text as string);
   191→      break;
   192→    case 'thinking_done':
   193→      callbacks.onThinkingDone();
   194→      break;
   195→    case 'tool_call':
   196→      callbacks.onToolCall({
   197→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   198→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   199→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   200→        command: (data.command as string) ?? undefined,
   201→        toolInput: (data.input as Record<string, unknown>) ?? undefined,
   202→        columns: [],
   203→        rows: [],
   204→        rowCount: 0,
   205→      });
   206→      break;
   207→    case 'tool_result': {
   208→      const result: ToolCallResult = {
   209→        toolCallId: (data.id as string) ?? '',
   210→        toolName: (data.name as string) ?? undefined,
   211→        sql: (data.sql as string) ?? '',
   212→        columns: (data.columns as string[]) ?? [],
   213→        rows: (data.rows as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [],
   214→        rowCount: (data.rowCount as number) ?? 0,
   215→        error: (data.error as string) ?? undefined,
   216→        output: (data.output as string) ?? undefined,
   217→        rawContent: (data.content as string) ?? undefined,
   218→        chart_spec: (data.chart_spec as { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown> }) ?? undefined,
   219→      };
   220→      callbacks.onToolResult(result);
   221→      break;
   222→    }
   223→    case 'subagent_start':
   224→      callbacks.onSubagentStart?.({
   225→        id: data.id as string,
   226→        name: data.name as string,
   227→        prompt: (data.prompt as string) ?? '',
   228→      });
   229→      break;
   230→    case 'subagent_end': {
   231→      type ChartSpec = { data: unknown[]; layout?: Record<string, unknown>; frames?: unknown[] };
   232→      // Backend sends chart_specs (array) for multi-chart support.
   233→      // Fall back to legacy chart_spec (single object) for backwards compat.
   234→      const rawSpecs = data.chart_specs as ChartSpec[] | undefined;
   235→      const legacySpec = data.chart_spec as ChartSpec | undefined;
   236→      const chartSpecs: ChartSpec[] = rawSpecs ?? (legacySpec ? [legacySpec] : []);
   237→
   238→      if (chartSpecs.length > 0) {
   239→        // Emit one callback per chart so each gets its own segment
   240→        for (const spec of chartSpecs) {
   241→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   242→            id: data.id as string,
   243→            name: data.name as string,
   244→            chart_spec: spec,
   245→          });
   246→        }
   247→        // Emit text result separately (if any) so it doesn't get lost
   248→        if (data.result) {
   249→          callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   250→            id: data.id as string,
   251→            name: data.name as string,
   252→            result: data.result as string,
   253→          });
   254→        }
   255→      } else {
   256→        callbacks.onSubagentEnd?.({
   257→          id: data.id as string,
   258→          name: data.name as string,
   259→          result: (data.result as string) ?? undefined,
   260→          sql_results: (data.sql_results as Array<{ sql: string; columns?: string[]; rows?: Record<string, unknown>[]; rowCount?: number; error?: string }>) ?? undefined,
   261→        });
   262→      }
   263→      break;
   264→    }
   265→    case 'user_question': {
   266→      // Normalize options: the agent may send plain strings or {label, description} objects
   267→      const rawOptions = (data.options as unknown[]) ?? [];
   268→      const normalizedOptions: UserQuestionOption[] = (Array.isArray(rawOptions) ? rawOptions : []).map(
   269→        (opt: unknown) => {
   270→          if (typeof opt === 'string') return { label: opt };
   271→          if (typeof opt === 'object' && opt !== null && 'label' in opt) return opt as UserQuestionOption;
   272→          return { label: String(opt) };
   273→        }
   274→      );
   275→      callbacks.onUserQuestion?.({
   276→        questionId: data.question_id as string,
   277→        question: data.question as string,
   278→        options: normalizedOptions,
   279→        multiSelect: (data.multi_select as boolean) ?? false,
   280→      });
   281→      break;
   282→    }
   283→    case 'done':
   284→      callbacks.onDone((data.session_id as string) ?? null);
   285→      break;
   286→    case 'error':
   287→      callbacks.onError((data.message as string) ?? 'Unknown error');
   288→      break;
   289→  }
   290→}
   291→
```

     1→import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
     2→import { useTranslation } from '../hooks/useTranslation';
     3→import './ConversationHistory.css';
     4→
     5→interface Conversation {
     6→  id: string;
     7→  title: string | null;
     8→  created_at: string;
     9→  updated_at: string;
    10→}
    11→
    12→interface ConversationHistoryProps {
    13→  activeConversationId: string | null;
    14→  onSelect: (conversationId: string) => void;
    15→  onNew: () => void;
    16→  onDelete: (conversationId: string) => void;
    17→  onRename: (conversationId: string, title: string) => void;
    18→  refreshTrigger: number;
    19→  sessionId: string;
    20→}
    21→
    22→function timeAgo(dateStr: string, t: (key: string, params?: Record<string, string | number>) => string): string {
    23→  const date = new Date(dateStr);
    24→  const now = new Date();
    25→  const seconds = Math.floor((now.getTime() - date.getTime()) / 1000);
    26→  if (seconds < 60) return t('timeJustNow');
    27→  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    28→  if (minutes < 60) return t('timeMinutesAgo', { count: minutes });
    29→  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
    30→  if (hours < 24) return t('timeHoursAgo', { count: hours });
    31→  const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);
    32→  if (days < 30) return t('timeDaysAgo', { count: days });
    33→  return date.toLocaleDateString();
    34→}
    35→
    36→export function ConversationHistory({
    37→  activeConversationId,
    38→  onSelect,
    39→  onNew,
    40→  onDelete,
    41→  onRename,
    42→  refreshTrigger,
    43→  sessionId,
    44→}: ConversationHistoryProps) {
    45→  const { t } = useTranslation();
    46→  const [conversations, setConversations] = useState<Conversation[]>([]);
    47→  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    48→  const [editTitle, setEditTitle] = useState('');
    49→  const editInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    50→
    51→  // Tick state to force re-render of relative timestamps every 60s
    52→  const [, setTick] = useState(0);
    53→  useEffect(() => {
    54→    const interval = setInterval(() => setTick((t) => t + 1), 60_000);
    55→    return () => clearInterval(interval);
    56→  }, []);
    57→
    58→  const fetchConversations = useCallback(async () => {
    59→    try {
    60→      const res = await fetch('/api/conversations', {
    61→        headers: { 'X-Session-ID': sessionId },
    62→      });
    63→      if (res.ok) {
    64→        setConversations(await res.json());
    65→      }
    66→    } catch {
    67→      // Sidebar fetch failure: show empty list
    68→    }
    69→  }, [sessionId]);
    70→
    71→  useEffect(() => {
    72→    fetchConversations();
    73→  }, [fetchConversations, refreshTrigger]);
    74→
    75→  useEffect(() => {
    76→    if (editingId && editInputRef.current) {
    77→      editInputRef.current.focus();
    78→      editInputRef.current.select();
    79→    }
    80→  }, [editingId]);
    81→
    82→  const handleRenameSubmit = (id: string) => {
    83→    const trimmed = editTitle.trim();
    84→    if (trimmed) {
    85→      onRename(id, trimmed);
    86→    }
    87→    setEditingId(null);
    88→  };
    89→
    90→  const handleDelete = (e: React.MouseEvent, id: string) => {
    91→    e.stopPropagation();
    92→    if (confirm(t('deleteConversationConfirm'))) {
    93→      onDelete(id);
    94→    }
    95→  };
    96→
    97→  const handleStartRename = (e: React.MouseEvent, id: string, currentTitle: string) => {
    98→    e.stopPropagation();
    99→    setEditingId(id);
   100→    setEditTitle(currentTitle || '');
   101→  };
   102→
   103→  return (
   104→    <div className="conv-history">
   105→      <div className="conv-history__header">
   106→        <span className="conv-history__title">{t('conversations')}</span>
   107→        <button className="conv-history__new-btn" onClick={onNew} title={t('newConversation')}>
   108→          +
   109→        </button>
   110→      </div>
   111→      <div className="conv-history__list">
   112→        {conversations.length === 0 ? (
   113→          <div className="conv-history__empty">{t('noConversationsYet')}</div>
   114→        ) : (
   115→          conversations.map((conv) => (
   116→            <div
   117→              key={conv.id}
   118→              className={`conv-history__item ${conv.id === activeConversationId ? 'conv-history__item--active' : ''}`}
   119→              onClick={() => onSelect(conv.id)}
   120→            >
   121→              {editingId === conv.id ? (
   122→                <input
   123→                  ref={editInputRef}
   124→                  className="conv-history__edit-input"
   125→                  value={editTitle}
   126→                  onChange={(e) => setEditTitle(e.target.value)}
   127→                  onBlur={() => handleRenameSubmit(conv.id)}
   128→                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
   129→                    if (e.key === 'Enter') handleRenameSubmit(conv.id);
   130→                    if (e.key === 'Escape') setEditingId(null);
   131→                  }}
   132→                  onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   133→                />
   134→              ) : (
   135→                <>
   136→                  <div className="conv-history__item-title">
   137→                    {conv.title || t('untitled')}

    98→    } else {

   155→    </div>
   156→  );

    13→    content: string;

    18→
    19→interface ConversationContextType {
    20→  activeConversationId: string | null;
    21→  refreshTrigger: number;
    22→  createConversation: (firstMessage: string) => Promise<string>;
    23→  selectConversation: (id: string) => Promise<ChatMessage[]>;
    24→  deleteConversation: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
    25→  renameConversation: (id: string, title: string) => Promise<void>;
    26→  startNewConversation: () => void;
    27→  triggerRefresh: () => void;
    28→}
    29→
    30→const ConversationContext = createContext<ConversationContextType | null>(null);
    31→
    32→export function ConversationProvider({ children, sessionId }: { children: ReactNode; sessionId: string }) {
    33→  const [activeConversationId, setActiveConversationId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    34→  const [refreshTrigger, setRefreshTrigger] = useState(0);
    35→
    36→  const triggerRefresh = useCallback(() => {
    37→    setRefreshTrigger((n) => n + 1);
    38→  }, []);
    39→
    40→  const createConversation = useCallback(async (firstMessage: string): Promise<string> => {
    41→    const title = firstMessage.length > 50 ? firstMessage.slice(0, 50) + '...' : firstMessage;
    42→    const res = await fetch('/api/conversations', {
    43→      method: 'POST',
    44→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-Session-ID': sessionId },
    45→      body: JSON.stringify({ title }),
    46→    });
    47→    const conv = await res.json();
    48→    setActiveConversationId(conv.id);
    49→    triggerRefresh();
    50→    return conv.id;
    51→  }, [triggerRefresh, sessionId]);
    52→
    53→  const selectConversation = useCallback(async (id: string): Promise<ChatMessage[]> => {
    54→    const res = await fetch(`/api/conversations/${id}`);
    55→    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to load conversation');
    56→    const conv: Conversation = await res.json();
    57→    setActiveConversationId(id);
    58→
    59→    return buildChatMessages(conv.messages || []);
    60→  }, []);
    61→
    62→  const deleteConversation = useCallback(async (id: string) => {
    63→    await fetch(`/api/conversations/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    64→    if (activeConversationId === id) {
    65→      setActiveConversationId(null);
    66→    }
    67→    triggerRefresh();
    68→  }, [activeConversationId, triggerRefresh]);
    69→
    70→  const renameConversation = useCallback(async (id: string, title: string) => {
    71→    await fetch(`/api/conversations/${id}`, {
    72→      method: 'PUT',
    73→      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    74→      body: JSON.stringify({ title }),
    75→    });
    76→    triggerRefresh();
    77→  }, [triggerRefresh]);
    78→
    79→  const startNewConversation = useCallback(() => {
    80→    setActiveConversationId(null);
    81→  }, []);
    82→
    83→  return (
    84→    <ConversationContext.Provider
    85→      value={{
    86→        activeConversationId,
    87→        refreshTrigger,
    88→        createConversation,
    89→        selectConversation,
    90→        deleteConversation,
    91→        renameConversation,
    92→        startNewConversation,
    93→        triggerRefresh,
    94→      }}
    95→    >
    96→      {children}
    97→    </ConversationContext.Provider>
    98→  );
    99→}

   104→  return ctx;
   105→}

> AGENT

Good, I have all the context I need. Let me now present the design section by section.

---

## Design Section 1: Per-Conversation Stream State

The core change: replace the singleton refs with a `Map<conversationId, StreamState>`.

**New type:**
```typescript
interface StreamState {
  assistantId: string;
  segments: ContentSegment[];
  currentText: string;
  textBuffer: string;
  phase: 'thinking' | 'answer';
  abortController: AbortController;
  sessionId: string | null;
  // The messages array for this conversation, updated live
  messages: ChatMessage[];
}
```

**Key changes to `AgentContext`:**
- `streamStatesRef = useRef<Map<string, StreamState>>(new Map())` — holds active streams
- The current `messages` state stays: it shows whichever conversation is active
- `isStreaming` becomes derived: `true` if the active conversation has an entry in `streamStatesRef`
- Remove the generation counter — no longer needed since each stream writes to its own `StreamState`, not to shared refs
- `streamingConversationsRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set())` — tracks all conversations with active streams (for sidebar indicator)

**How callbacks work:**
Each `sendMessage` call creates a `StreamState` for that `conversationId`, puts it in the map, and passes callbacks that write to **that specific StreamState**. The callbacks close over the `StreamState` object (not shared refs), so they never conflict.

**How messages get to the screen:**
- If `activeConversationId === streamState.conversationId`, the callback also calls `setMessages(...)` to update the display live.
- If the user has switched away, the callback still updates `streamState.messages` but does NOT call `setMessages` — the data accumulates silently.
- When the user switches back, `loadMessages` reads from `streamState.messages` (if stream is still active) or from the backend (if stream completed).

Does this core model look right so far?

> DEVELOPER

yes

> AGENT

## Design Section 2: Conversation Switching (Re-attachment)

**When user switches FROM conversation A TO conversation B:**

1. Snapshot A's current `messages` state into `streamStatesRef.get(A).messages` (if A has an active stream) or `messagesCacheRef` (if not streaming)
2. Check if B has an active stream in `streamStatesRef`:
   - **Yes (re-attach):** Read `streamStatesRef.get(B).messages` and set as `messages` state. Future callbacks for B now also call `setMessages` since B is active again. Live text resumes immediately.
   - **No (load from backend):** Fetch from `/api/conversations/B` as today, or use `messagesCacheRef` if cached.
3. Update `activeConversationIdRef` to B.

**How callbacks know whether to update the screen:**

Each callback checks `activeConversationIdRef.current === myConversationId`. This is a ref read (not state), so it's always current. No generation counter needed.

```
callback(data) {
  // Always update the stream's own state
  streamState.messages = updatedMessages;
  streamState.segments = updatedSegments;
  // Only push to React state if this conversation is being viewed
  if (activeConversationIdRef.current === conversationId) {
    setMessages(streamState.messages);
  }
}
```

**When a stream completes (onDone):**
1. Remove from `streamStatesRef` and `streamingConversationsRef`
2. If user is viewing this conversation, update `setMessages` with final state and `setIsStreaming(false)`
3. If user is NOT viewing it, the backend has persisted the response. Delete any stale cache entry so next switch fetches fresh data.

Does this switching/re-attachment model look right?

> DEVELOPER

ok

> AGENT

## Design Section 3: Multiple Simultaneous Streams

**Current blocker:** `sendMessage` has `if (isStreaming) return;` — it rejects new messages while any stream is active.

**Change:** `isStreaming` becomes per-conversation. The `ChatInput` should only be disabled if the **active conversation** is streaming, not if some background conversation is.

```typescript
// Derived from streamStatesRef
const isActiveStreaming = activeConversationId 
  ? streamStatesRef.current.has(activeConversationId)
  : false;
```

**`sendMessage` guard changes:**
- Remove the global `if (isStreaming) return;`
- Add: `if (conversationId && streamStatesRef.current.has(conversationId)) return;` — prevent double-sending in the same conversation, but allow sending in a different one.

**Shared refs that need per-stream isolation:**
- `abortRef` — moves into `StreamState`
- `textBufferRef`, `flushTimerRef` — moves into `StreamState`
- `assistantIdRef`, `segmentsRef`, `currentTextRef`, `phaseRef` — moves into `StreamState`
- `sessionIdRef` — moves into `StreamState`
- `messagesRef` — stays global (tracks what's currently displayed)

**`flushText` becomes per-stream:**
Currently it reads from shared `textBufferRef` and `assistantIdRef`. It needs to accept a `StreamState` parameter instead:
```typescript
function flushTextForStream(state: StreamState) {
  if (!state.textBuffer) return;
  state.currentText += state.textBuffer;
  // update state.messages with the new text
  state.textBuffer = '';
  // if active, also setMessages(...)
}
```

Does this multi-stream approach look right?

> DEVELOPER

yes

> AGENT

## Design Section 4: Sidebar Streaming Indicator

**How the sidebar knows which conversations are streaming:**

Add a new piece of state exposed from `AgentContext`:
```typescript
streamingConversationIds: Set<string>
```

This is a React state (not just a ref) because the sidebar needs to re-render when it changes. Updated when:
- `sendMessage` starts a stream: add conversationId to the set
- `onDone`/`onError` fires: remove conversationId from the set

**ConversationHistory receives it as a prop:**
```typescript
interface ConversationHistoryProps {
  // ... existing props
  streamingConversationIds: Set<string>;
}
```

**Visual indicator:** A small pulsing dot next to the conversation title, using a CSS animation. Simple and lightweight:
```css
.conv-history__streaming-dot {
  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--accent-color);
  animation: pulse 1.5s ease-in-out infinite;
}
```

**Prop threading:** `App.tsx` passes `streamingConversationIds` down through `Sidebar` to `ConversationHistory`. This requires adding it to the `AgentContextValue` interface.

Does the sidebar indicator approach look right?

> DEVELOPER

yes

> AGENT

## Design Section 5: `editMessage` and `respondToQuestion` Adjustments

**`editMessage`:** Currently uses the same shared refs pattern. It needs to:
- Create a new `StreamState` for the conversation (same as `sendMessage`)
- The edit truncates messages and starts a fresh stream, so it replaces any existing `StreamState` for that conversation (abort the old one first)

**`respondToQuestion`:** Currently writes to `segmentsRef` and `assistantIdRef`. It needs to look up the correct `StreamState` by conversation ID to find the right segments/assistantId. The `questionId` is unique, so we can scan across active streams if needed, but the simpler approach is to have `respondToQuestion` accept a `conversationId` parameter.

**`deleteMessage`:** Only works when not streaming in the active conversation. No change needed beyond checking per-conversation streaming instead of global.

Does this look right?

> DEVELOPER

yes
